<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 3</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى بْنُ يَحْيَى، أَخْبَرَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ مُسْلِمٍ الطَّائِفِيُّ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْحُوَيْرِثِ، مَوْلَى آلِ السَّائِبِ أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عَبَّاسٍ، قَالَ ذَهَبَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِلَى الْغَائِطِ فَلَمَّا جَاءَ قُدِّمَ لَهُ طَعَامٌ فَقِيلَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ أَلاَ تَوَضَّأُ ‏.‏ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لِمَ أَلِلصَّلاَةِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah in Ibn Abbas account. The complete field supplies the food/ablution exchange and explicitly names the answering speaker.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn &#x27;Abbas reported: The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) went to the privy and when he came back, he was presented with food. It was said to him; Messenger of Allah, wouldn&#x27;t you perform ablution. He said: Why, am I to say prayer?</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 829]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/829" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">मुहम्मद बिन मुस्लिम ताईफ़ी ने अम्र बिन दीनार से, उन्होंने आले साहेब के आज़ाद करदा ग़ुलाम सईद बिन हुवैरिस से रिवायत की के उसने अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) को ये कहते हुए सुना: अल्लाह के रसूल (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) क़ज़ाए हाजत के लिए तशरीफ़ ले गये, जब आप आये तो आपको खाना पेश किया गया, आप से अर्ज़ की गई: ऐ अल्लाह के रसूल! क्या आप वुज़ू नहीं फ़रमायेंगे? आप ने जवाब दिया: किस लिए? क्या नमाज़ के लिए?</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/829" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">محمد بن مسلم طائفی نے عمرو بن دینار سے ، انہوں نے آل سائب کے آزاد کردہ غلام سعید بن حویرث سے روایت کی کہ اس نے عبد اللہ بن عباس ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ کو ( یہ ) کہتے ہوئے سنا : اللہ کے رسولﷺ قضائے حاجت کے لیے تشریف لے گئے ، جب آپ آئے تو آپ کو کھانا پیش کیا گیا ، آپ سے عرض کی گئی : اے اللہ کے رسول! کیا آپ وضو نہیں فرمائیں گئے؟ آپ نے جواب دیا : ’’کس لیے؟ کیا نماز کے لیے؟ ‘ ‘</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/829" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 797

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A830)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 3</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ عَمْرِو بْنِ عَبَّادِ بْنِ جَبَلَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو عَاصِمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ حُوَيْرِثٍ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ ابْنَ عَبَّاسٍ، يَقُولُ إِنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَضَى حَاجَتَهُ مِنَ الْخَلاَءِ فَقُرِّبَ إِلَيْهِ طَعَامٌ فَأَكَلَ وَلَمْ يَمَسَّ مَاءً ‏.‏ قَالَ وَزَادَنِي عَمْرُو بْنُ دِينَارٍ عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْحُوَيْرِثِ أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قِيلَ لَهُ إِنَّكَ لَمْ تَوَضَّأْ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَا أَرَدْتُ صَلاَةً فَأَتَوَضَّأَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَزَعَمَ عَمْرٌو أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ مِنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْحُوَيْرِثِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 830]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/830" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn Abbas, reported: The Apostle of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) came out of the privy after relieving himself, and food was brought to him and he took it, and did not touch water. In another narration transmitted by Sa&#x27;id b. al-Huwairith it is like this: It was said to the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) You have not performed ablution. He said: I do not intend to say prayer that I should perform ablution</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 830]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/830" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 798

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A831)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 3</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى بْنُ يَحْيَى، أَخْبَرَنَا حَمَّادُ بْنُ زَيْدٍ، وَقَالَ، يَحْيَى أَيْضًا أَخْبَرَنَا هُشَيْمٌ، كِلاَهُمَا عَنْ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ بْنِ صُهَيْبٍ، عَنْ أَنَسٍ، - فِي حَدِيثِ حَمَّادٍ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا دَخَلَ الْخَلاَءَ وَفِي حَدِيثِ هُشَيْمٍ - أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَ إِذَا دَخَلَ الْكَنِيفَ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَعُوذُ بِكَ مِنَ الْخُبْثِ وَالْخَبَائِثِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 831]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/831" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 831</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   831 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Anas reported: When the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) entered the privy, and in the hadith transmitted by Hushaim (the words are): When the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) entered the lavatory, be used to say: O Allah, I seek refuge in Thee from wicked and noxious things</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 831]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/831" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 831</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   831 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">याहया बिन याह्या ने हम्द बिन ज़ैद और हूशैम से अन्हु) से रिवायत की (हम्मद की हैदिथ में है: रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) जब बैत-उल-खला में दाखिल होते और हूशैम के अलफ़ाज़ हैं: जब किसी औत वाली जगह में दाखिल होते) तो फ़रमाते: ऐ अल्लाह! मैं नर और मादा दोनों क़िस्म की खाबीत मख्लूक़ से तेरी पनाह में आता हूँ.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 831]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/831" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 831</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   831 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ بن یحییٰ نے حماد بن زید اور ہشیم سے ، ان دونوں نے عبد العزیز بن صہیب سے ، انہوں نے حضرت انس ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے روایت کی ( حماد کی حدیث میں ہے : رسول اللہﷺ جب بیت الخلاء میں داخل ہوتے اور ہشیم کے الفاظ ہیں : جب کسی اوٹ والی جگہ میں داخل ہوتے ) تو فرماتے : ’’اے اللہ! میں نر اور مادہ دونوں قسم کی خبیث مخلوق سے تیری پناہ میں آتا ہوں ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 831]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/831" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya bin Yahya ne Hammad bin Zaid aur Hushaim se, un dono ne Abdul Aziz bin Suhaib se, unhone Hazrat Anas (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat ki (Hammad ki hadith mein hai: Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) jab bait-ul-khala mein dakhil hote aur Hushaim ke alfaaz hain: jab kisi aut wali jagah mein dakhil hote) to farmate: Ae Allah! Main nar aur mada dono qisam ki khabeeth makhlooq se teri panah mein aata hoon.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 831]</p>

<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي زُهَيْرُ بْنُ حَرْبٍ، حَدَّثَنَا إِسْمَاعِيلُ ابْنُ عُلَيَّةَ، ح وَحَدَّثَنَا شَيْبَانُ بْنُ فَرُّوخَ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الْوَارِثِ، كِلاَهُمَا عَنْ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ، عَنْ أَنَسٍ، قَالَ أُقِيمَتِ الصَّلاَةُ وَرَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَجِيٌّ لِرَجُلٍ - وَفِي حَدِيثِ عَبْدِ الْوَارِثِ وَنَبِيُّ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يُنَاجِي الرَّجُلَ - فَمَا قَامَ إِلَى الصَّلاَةِ حَتَّى نَامَ الْقَوْمُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/833" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This reading preserves separate source spans. Intervening source notes are retained under Comments. Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah in the episode reported by Anas. The source supplies the waiting congregation, the private conversation and the delayed start of prayer, with a second wording of the same episode. Preserve both passages in source order; the transmitter-comparison introduction remains context. Some reports contain separate source passages. Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Anas reported: (The people) stood up for prayer and the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) was whispering to a man</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr"> The Apostle of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) was having a private conversation with a man, and did not start the prayer till the people dozed off</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 833]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इस्माइल बिन उलैयह और अब्दुल वरिठ दोनों ने अब्दुल अज़ीज़ से नमाज़ के लिए तकबीर कह दी गयी और रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) एक आदमी से बहुत करीब हो कर आहिस्ता आहिस्ता बात कर रहे थे. (अब्दुल वरिठ की रिवायत में रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) नाजी ली राजुल के बजाये नबी अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) युनाजी-ुर-राजुल आप एक आदमी से आहिस्ता आहिस्ता बातें कर रहे थे है. मफ़हूम एक है) तो आप नमाज़ के लिए खड़े नहीं हुए यहाँ तक के लोग (बैठे बैठे) सो गए.</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">اسماعیل بن علیہ اور عبد الوارث دونوں نے عبد العزیز سے ، انہوں نے حضرت انس ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے روایت کی ، کہا : نماز کے لیے تکبیر کہہ دی گئی اور رسول اللہﷺ ایک آدمی سے بہت قریب ہو کر آہستہ آہستہ بات کر رہے تھے ۔ ( عبد الوارث کی روایت میں ورسول الله ﷺ نجي لرجل کے بجائے ونبی اللہ ﷺ یناجی الرجل آپ ایک آدمی سے آہستہ آہستہ باتیں کر رہے تھے ہے ۔ مفہوم ایک ہے ) تو آپ نماز کے لیے کھڑے نہیں ہوئے یہاں تک کہ لوگ ( بیٹھےبیٹھے ) سو گئے ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Ismail bin Ulayyah aur Abdul Warith dono ne Abdul Aziz se, unhone Hazrat Anas (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat ki, kaha: Namaz ke liye takbeer keh di gayi aur Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ek aadmi se bahut qareeb ho kar aahista aahista baat kar rahe the. (Abdul Warith ki riwayat mein Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) naji li rajul ke bajaye Nabi Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) yunaaji-ur-rajul aap ek aadmi se aahista aahista baatein kar rahe the hai. Mafhoom ek hai) to aap namaz ke liye khade nahi hue yahan tak ke log (baithe baithe) so gaye.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/833" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>
